A list of **155+** LLM services, tools, and infrastructure for running AI locally. Criteria for inclusion:

* Open Source
* Self-hostable
* Friendly to containerization (Docker, Podman, etc.)
* Relates to homelab or personal AI use cases
* Well-documented with setup instructions

Relevance score (<img src="./assets/rocket.svg" width="16" height="16" style="vertical-align: middle;"> 0–100%): a composite metric of **Popularity** (logarithm of stars) and **Recency** (exponential decay with a 90-day half-life). This highlights projects that are both widely recognized and actively maintained.

Last maintenance review: **2026-11-11**. Project links, licensing, self-hosting instructions for new submissions, and upstream activity were checked. Archived projects and projects without commits for over a year were removed. Section counts include services listed in more than one section.

Added in this review: [Atlas UI 3](https://github.com/sandialabs/atlas-ui-3) ⭐ 47 | 🐛 39 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-11-13, [ENZO](https://github.com/theguysudo/ENZO) ⭐ 121 | 🐛 5 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-11-13, [vimin-core](https://github.com/pberlizov/vimin-public) ⭐ 0 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-07-05, [LLMKube](https://github.com/defilantech/LLMKube) ⭐ 266 | 🐛 144 | 🌐 Go | 📅 2026-11-13, [CorvinOS](https://github.com/CorvinLabs/CorvinOS) ⭐ 25 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-11-12, [OpenAgent](https://github.com/the-open-agent/openagent) ⭐ 5,742 | 🐛 52 | 🌐 Go | 📅 2026-11-06, [llm-swarm-router](https://github.com/matthewdcage/llm-swarm-router) ⭐ 27 | 🐛 75 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-11-11, [Routerly](https://github.com/Inebrio/Routerly) ⭐ 112 | 🐛 12 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-11-12.

## Contributing

This list is auto-generated from [Harbor's service metadata](https://github.com/av/harbor) ⭐ 3,277 | 🐛 6 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-11-11 and [locally reviewed submissions](./assets/curation.json).

To propose a service, [open an issue](https://github.com/av/awesome-llm-services/issues/new) with its source repository, license, and self-hosting instructions. Accepted submissions are kept in the local curation file so daily regeneration preserves them.

To regenerate: install [Deno](https://deno.com/), then run `deno run -A assets/generate.ts` from the repository root.
